.
∎

5.4.2
Estimates for the Bianchi pair
(\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)

Proposition 5.29
.

We have the following estimate for all
0\leq p\leq s+1
:

pB_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+EF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

(5.57)

Proof.

We recall from Proposition
5.26
that

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha)+\frac{1}{2}\tr\underline{\chi}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha)

\displaystyle=-2\not{d}_{2}^{*}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)+(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2))^{(1)}+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2))^{(1)},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)+2\tr\chi(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)

\displaystyle=\not{d}_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha)+(\mathbb{O}_{s-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2))^{(1)}.

Applying Proposition
5.18
with
\psi_{(1)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha
,
\psi_{(2)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta
,
a_{(1)}=\frac{1}{2}
,
a_{(2)}=2
,
h_{(1)}=(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2))^{(1)}+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2))^{(1)}
,
h_{(2)}=(\mathbb{O}_{s-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2))^{(1)}
and
0\leq p\leq s+1
, we obtain from (
5.9
) and Remark
5.2

\displaystyle pB_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+EF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1})+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)].

Applying Theorem
5.27
, we obtain for
0\leq p\leq s+1

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)]\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

Hence, we have for
0\leq p\leq s+1

pB_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+EF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
5.29
.
∎

5.4.3
Estimates for the Bianchi pair
(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,-\sigma))

Proposition 5.30
.

We have the following estimate for
0\leq p\leq 2
:

BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

(5.58)

Proof.

We recall from Proposition
5.26

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)+\tr\underline{\chi}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)

\displaystyle=-\not{d}_{1}^{*}(\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,-\sigma))+(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2))^{(1)}

\displaystyle+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2))^{(1)},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,-\sigma))+\frac{3}{2}\tr\chi(\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,-\sigma))

\displaystyle=\not{d}_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta)+(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2))^{(1)}.

Applying Proposition
5.18
with
\psi_{(1)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta
,
\psi_{(2)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,-\sigma)
,
a_{(1)}=1
,
a_{(2)}=\frac{3}{2}
,
h_{(1)}=(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2))^{(1)}+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2))^{(1)}
,
h_{(2)}=(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2))^{(1)}
and
0\leq p\leq 2
and combining with Proposition
5.29
, we obtain

\displaystyle BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1})+B_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\;\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\;\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)].

Applying Theorem
5.27
in corresponding cases, we infer for
0\leq p\leq 2

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{E}_{2s-{\delta_{B}}}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)]\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

Hence, we deduce for
0\leq p\leq 2

BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
5.30
.
∎

5.4.4
Estimates for the Bianchi pair
(\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,\sigma),\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})

Proposition 5.31
.

We have the following estimate for
0\leq p\leq 1
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})\\
\lesssim\;&E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.\end{split}

(5.59)

Proof.

We recall from Proposition
5.26

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}(\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,\sigma))+\frac{3}{2}\tr\underline{\chi}(\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,\sigma))

\displaystyle=-\not{d}_{1}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2))^{(1)},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})+\tr\chi(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})

\displaystyle=\not{d}_{1}^{*}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho)+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2))^{(1)}+(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2))^{(1)}.

Applying Proposition
5.18
with
\psi_{(1)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}(\rho,\sigma)
,
\psi_{(2)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}
,
a_{(1)}=1
,
a_{(2)}=\frac{3}{2}
,
h_{(1)}=(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2))^{(1)}
,
h_{(2)}=(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2))^{(1)}+(\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2))^{(1)}
and
0\leq p\leq 1
and combining with Proposition
5.30
, we obtain

\displaystyle BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\;\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)].

Applying Theorem
5.27
in corresponding cases, we deduce for
0\leq p\leq 1

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{s+1}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2)]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{-1}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{s+2}(s+2)]\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

Thus, we obtain for
0\leq p\leq 1

\displaystyle BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
5.31
.
∎

5.4.5
Estimates for the Bianchi pair
(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta},\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha})

Proposition 5.32
.

We have the following estimate:

BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})+F_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma,\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

(5.60)

Proof.

We have from Proposition
5.26

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})+2\tr\underline{\chi}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})

\displaystyle=-\not{d}_{2}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha})+(\mathbb{O}_{1}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2))^{(1)},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha})+\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha})

\displaystyle=2\not{d}_{2}^{*}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta})+(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2))^{(1)}.

Applying Proposition
5.18
with
\psi_{(1)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}
,
\psi_{(2)}=\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}
,
a_{(1)}=2
,
a_{(2)}=\frac{1}{2}
,
h_{(1)}=(\mathbb{O}_{1}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2))^{(1)}
,
h_{(2)}=(\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2))^{(1)}
and
p=0
and combining with Proposition
5.31

\displaystyle BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})+F_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}](u_{1},u_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;E_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma,\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\;\mathcal{N}_{0}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{1}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}^{1}_{0}[\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2)].

Applying Theorem
5.27
in corresponding cases, we obtain

\mathcal{N}_{0}^{1}[\mathbb{F}_{2}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{1}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2)]+\mathcal{N}^{1}_{0}[\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2),\mathbb{O}_{s}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{0}(s+2)]\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

Hence, we infer

\displaystyle BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})+F_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma,\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
5.32
.
∎

5.4.6
u
–decay estimates

Combining Propositions
5.29
–
5.32
, we have the following proposition.

Proposition 5.33
.

We have the following estimates:

•

We have for
0\leq p\leq s+1

\displaystyle\begin{split}&pB_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+EF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\\
\lesssim\;&E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.\end{split}

(5.61)

•

We have for
0\leq p\leq 2

\displaystyle\begin{split}&BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\wideparen{\rho},\not{\nabla}_{T}\wideparen{\sigma}](u_{1},u_{2})\\
\lesssim\;&E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.\end{split}

(5.62)

•

We have for
0\leq p\leq 1

\displaystyle\begin{split}&BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})\\
\lesssim\;&E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}}.\end{split}

(5.63)

•

We have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})+F_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}](u_{1},u_{2})\\
\lesssim\;&E_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\wideparen{\rho},\not{\nabla}_{T}\wideparen{\sigma},\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.\end{split}

(5.64)

Proposition 5.34
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle EF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha,\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma,\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})+F_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

Proof.

The proof is largely analogous to Proposition
5.23
. So, we only provide a sketch.

Recalling that

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha=r^{-1}O(\alpha_{4},\alpha^{(1)},\beta^{(1)}),

we have from Proposition
5.22

BEF_{s+2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim BEF_{s}[\alpha_{4}](u_{1},u_{2})+BEF_{s}^{1}[\alpha,\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

(5.65)

Taking
u_{1}=2^{j}
and
u_{2}=2^{j+1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{2^{j}}^{2^{j+1}}E_{s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u)du\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

which implies that there exists a sequence
u^{(j)}\in[2^{j},2^{j+1}]
such that

\displaystyle E_{s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u^{(j)})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u^{(j)}}.

Applying (
5.61
) with
p=s+1
,
u_{1}=u^{(j-1)}
and
u_{2}=u\in[2^{j},2^{j+1}]
, we deduce

\displaystyle BEF_{s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u^{(j-1)},u)\lesssim E_{s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u^{(j-1)})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u^{(j-1)}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u},

which implies

\displaystyle E_{s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u)\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u}.

Applying once again (
5.61
) with
p=s+1
, we deduce

\displaystyle BEF_{s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}}.

Next, proceeding as in Lemma
5.19
, we obtain

\displaystyle EF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

Combining with (
5.65
), we deduce

EF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\alpha](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\forall\;p\in[0,s+2].

(5.66)

Next, we have from (
5.61
), (
5.62
) and (
5.66
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad p\in[0,2],\\
E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{2})&\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\;p\in[0,2].\end{split}

(5.67)

Applying Lemma
5.20
18
18

18

In fact, we apply Lemma
5.20
by replacing
s
with
s+2
.
, we infer

\displaystyle BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

Moreover, we have from Corollary
4.11
and Proposition
5.22

\displaystyle BEF_{2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim BEF_{0}^{1}[\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}.

Hence, we obtain

BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\forall\;p\in[0,2].

(5.68)

Then, we have from (
5.63
), (
5.66
) and (
5.68
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\;\,\forall\;p\in[0,2],\\
E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{2})&\lesssim E_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\;\forall\;p\in[0,1].\end{split}

(5.69)

Applying Lemma
5.20
, we obtain

\displaystyle BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

Moreover, taking
p=2
in the first estimate in (
5.69
), we deduce

\displaystyle\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}E_{1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u)du\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}.

Proceeding as in Lemma
5.19
, we obtain a sequence
u^{(j)}\sim 2^{j}
satisfying

\displaystyle E_{1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u^{(j)})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{(u^{(j)})^{s+1}}.

Combining with the second estimate in (
5.69
), we deduce

\displaystyle E_{1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u)\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u^{s+1}}.

Hence, we have

BEF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\rho,\not{\nabla}_{T}\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\forall\;p\in[0,1].

(5.70)

Finally, we have from (
5.64
), (
5.66
), (
5.68
) and (
5.70
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}BF_{p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2-p}},\qquad\quad\forall\;p\in[0,1],\\
E_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{2})&\lesssim E_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.\end{split}

(5.71)

Applying Lemma
5.20
, we obtain

\displaystyle BEF_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\beta}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

(5.72)

Injecting it into (
5.64
), we infer

\displaystyle F_{0}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+2}}.

(5.73)

Combining the above estimates, this concludes the proof of Proposition
5.34
.
∎

5.5
End of the proof of Theorem
3.8

Proposition 5.35
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{D}_{s}[\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma]+\mathcal{D}_{s-1,1}[\underline{\beta}]+\mathcal{D}_{-1,s+1}[\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.\end{split}

(5.74)

Proof.

We have from Propositions
5.11
–
5.16

\displaystyle{{}^{e}\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{1}[\alpha,\beta,\wideparen{\rho},\wideparen{\sigma}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad\;{{}^{e}\mathcal{F}}_{s}^{1}[\underline{\beta}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad\;\;{{}^{e}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}^{1}[\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle{{}^{e}\mathcal{E}}_{0,s}^{1}[\underline{\beta}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad{{}^{e}\mathcal{E}}_{s}[\alpha_{4}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\;\,\,{{}^{e}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s+2}[\underline{\alpha}_{3}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Applying Propositions
4.16
and
5.3
, we deduce

\displaystyle{{}^{e}\mathcal{D}}_{s}[\alpha,\beta,\wideparen{\rho},\wideparen{\sigma}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{e}\mathcal{D}}_{s-1,1}[\underline{\beta}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{e}\mathcal{D}}_{-1,s+1}[\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Recalling (
3.3
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{p\in[2,4]}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\rho,\sigma)|_{p,S}&\lesssim\sup_{p\in[2,4]}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\wideparen{\rho},\wideparen{\sigma})|_{p,S}+|\Gamma_{g}|_{\infty,S}\sup_{p\in[2,4]}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{w}|_{p,S}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+3}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}r{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+3}{2}}},\end{split}

(5.75)

which implies

{{}^{e}\mathcal{D}}_{s}[\rho,\sigma]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Similarly, we have from Propositions
5.32
and
5.33
that in
{{}^{i}V}

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{E}}_{s}^{1}[\alpha,\beta,\wideparen{\rho},\wideparen{\sigma}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad\;{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{s}^{1}[\underline{\beta}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad\quad\;{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}^{1}[\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{E}}_{0,s}^{1}[\underline{\beta}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{E}}_{s}[\alpha_{4}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Recalling that
2T=e_{4}+e_{3}
and applying Propositions
4.16
and
5.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{s}[\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{s-1,1}[\underline{\beta}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-1,s+1}[\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

where we used (
5.75
). This concludes the proof of Proposition
5.35
.
∎

We then focus on the
u
–decay estimates in the interior region.

Definition 5.36
.

We define the truncation function on
\Sigma_{t}

\displaystyle f(x):=h\left(\frac{r(x)}{t}\right),

where
h
is a smooth cut-off function such that
h=1
for
s\leq\frac{1}{2}
and
h=0
for
s\geq\frac{3}{4}
. For any tensor field
X
on
\Sigma_{t}
, we denote:

\displaystyle\overset{\circ}{X}:=fX.

Proposition 5.37
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s+2}^{1}[\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta},\underline{\alpha}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta},\underline{\alpha}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Propositions
5.23
and
5.34

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}(\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta},\underline{\alpha})]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0.s}^{1}[\alpha,\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta},\underline{\alpha}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

which implies from (
2.22
) and (
2.23
)

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}E]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}H]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

We recall from Proposition
2.23

\displaystyle\sdiv E

\displaystyle=k\wedge H,

\displaystyle\sdiv H

\displaystyle=-k\wedge E,

\displaystyle\curl E

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}H-\nabla\log\phi\wedge E-\frac{1}{2}k\times H,

\displaystyle\curl H

\displaystyle=-\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}E-\nabla\log\phi\wedge H+\frac{1}{2}k\times E.

Commuting with
f
, we easily deduce
19
19

19

See (7.7.6g)–(7.7.6j) in
[
5
]
for the explicit formulae.

\displaystyle\sdiv\overset{\circ}{E}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\overset{\circ}{H}+\nabla f\cdot\overset{\circ}{E},

\displaystyle\sdiv\overset{\circ}{H}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\overset{\circ}{E}+\nabla f\cdot\overset{\circ}{H},

\displaystyle\curl\overset{\circ}{E}

\displaystyle=f\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}H+\Gamma_{w}\cdot(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})+\nabla f\cdot\overset{\circ}{E},

\displaystyle\curl\overset{\circ}{H}

\displaystyle=-f\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}E+\Gamma_{w}\cdot(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})+\nabla f\cdot\overset{\circ}{H}.

Applying Proposition
4.19
, we obtain
20
20

20

Notice from Proposition
2.21
that
\sRic=O(E)+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}
.

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla\overset{\circ}{E}|^{2}+|\nabla\overset{\circ}{H}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}f^{2}|\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}E,\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}H|^{2}+|\Gamma_{w}\cdot(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|^{2}+|\nabla f|^{2}|(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|^{2}+|(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}\left(r\leq\frac{3t}{4}\right)}|\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}E,\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{T}H|^{2}+\int_{\frac{t}{4}}^{t}r^{2}|r^{-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{-\frac{2}{4}}(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|_{4,S}^{2}du

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{r^{2}}{t^{2}}|r^{-\frac{2}{2}}(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|_{2,S}^{2}du+\int_{\frac{t}{4}}^{t}r^{2}|r^{-\frac{2}{3}}(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|^{3}_{3,S}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}+\int_{\frac{t}{4}}^{t}r^{2}\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{t^{s+1}t^{s+3}}du+\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{r^{s+3}}du+\int_{\frac{t}{4}}^{t}r^{2}\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{t^{\frac{3(s+3)}{2}}}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{t^{2s+1}}+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{t^{\frac{3s+5}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}.

Combining with Proposition
4.15
, we obtain

F_{-2}^{1}[\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H}]({{}^{i}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}.

Next, applying Proposition
4.16
, we infer

\displaystyle|(\overset{\circ}{E},\overset{\circ}{H})|_{4,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}.

Recalling Definition
5.36
, we deduce

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s+2}^{1}[E,H]({{}^{i}_{i}V})\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[E,H]({{}^{i}_{i}V})\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

The estimates in
{{}^{i}_{e}V}
follows directly from Propositions
5.23
and
5.34
. Combining with (
2.22
), this concludes the proof of Proposition
5.37
.
∎

5.6
Improved estimate for
\beta

In this section, we prove an improved estimate for
\beta
which will be used in Section
7.2
.

Lemma 5.38
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\sup_{p\in[-1,s]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{B}}_{p,s-p}^{1}[\beta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Theorem
3.8

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap{{}^{i}V}}r^{p}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},\qquad\forall-2\leq p\leq s.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{{}^{i}V}}r^{-2}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}dt\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{-2}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}+\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}dt\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap{{}^{i}_{e}V}}r^{-2}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{s+2}}+\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap{{}^{i}_{e}V}}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s+1}}.

Interpolating with

\displaystyle\int_{{}^{i}V}r^{s-1}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

which follows directly from Theorem
3.8
, this concludes the proof of Lemma
5.38
.
∎

Proposition 5.39
.

We have the following estimate:

{{}^{i}\mathcal{E}}_{-1,s+1}^{1}[\beta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We recall from Proposition
4.10

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{3}\beta+\tr\underline{\chi}\,\beta

\displaystyle=-\not{d}_{1}^{*}(\rho,-\sigma)+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\underline{\beta}+\Gamma_{b}\cdot\rho,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\rho,-\sigma)+\frac{3}{2}\tr\chi(\rho,-\sigma)

\displaystyle=\not{d}_{1}\beta+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\beta.

Applying Propositions
4.5
and
5.18
, we deduce for
-1\leq p\leq 0

\displaystyle EF_{p}^{1}[\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}^{1}[\rho,\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim B_{p}^{1}[\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+E_{p}^{1}[\beta](u_{1})+\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[R,\Gamma_{w}\cdot R]({{}^{i}V}),

(5.76)

where

R\in\{\beta,\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}\}.

We first have from Lemma
5.38
that for
-1\leq p\leq 0

\displaystyle B_{p}^{1}[\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

Moreover, we have from the proof of Proposition
5.12
that for
-1\leq p\leq 0

\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[R,\Gamma_{w}\cdot R]({{}^{i}_{e}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

We also have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{p}^{1}[R,\Gamma_{w}\cdot R]({{}^{i}_{i}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{{}^{i}_{i}V}r^{p}|R^{(1)}||\Gamma_{w}||R^{(1)}|+\int_{{}^{i}_{i}V}r^{p}|R^{(1)}||\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}||R|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|R^{(1)}|^{2}+\left(\int_{{}^{i}_{i}V}r^{2p}|R^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{{}^{i}_{i}V}|\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}|^{2}|R|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}+\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}dt\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{2p}|R^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{t^{2(s+1)}}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}+\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{t^{s-2p}}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

Injecting the above estimates into (
5.76
), we infer

\displaystyle EF_{p}^{1}[\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+BF_{p}^{1}[\rho,\sigma](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim E_{p}^{1}[\beta](u_{1})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(5.77)

We recall from Lemma
5.38

B_{0}^{1}[\beta](u_{1},u_{2})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}},

which implies that there exists a sequence
u^{(j)}\sim 2^{j}
satisfying

\displaystyle E_{-1}^{1}[\beta](u^{(j)})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{(u^{(j)})^{s+1}}.

Applying (
5.77
) with
u_{1}=u^{(j-1)}
,
u_{2}=u\in[2^{j},2^{j+1}]
and
p=-1
, we obtain

\displaystyle E_{-1}^{1}[\beta](u)\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u^{s+1}}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
5.39
.
∎

6
Maximal connection estimates (Theorem
3.9
)

In this section, we apply the elliptic estimates in Section
4.5
to prove Theorem
3.9
.

6.1
Preliminaries

Proposition 6.1
.

We have the following elliptic system on
\Sigma_{t}
:

\displaystyle\sdiv k

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\curl k

\displaystyle=H,

\displaystyle\tr k

\displaystyle=0.

Proof.

See (11.1.1a) in
[
5
]
.
∎

Proposition 6.2
.

We have the following system on
\Sigma_{t}
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\not{\sdiv}\,}\epsilon&=-\nabla_{N}\delta-\frac{3}{2}\tr\theta\,\delta+\widehat{\kappa}\cdot\widehat{\theta}-2(a^{-1}\not{\nabla}a)\cdot\epsilon,\\
{\not{\curl}\,}\epsilon&=\sigma+\widehat{\theta}\wedge\widehat{\kappa},\\
\not{\nabla}_{N}\epsilon+\tr\theta\,\epsilon&=\frac{1}{2}(\beta-\underline{\beta})+\not{\nabla}\delta-2\widehat{\theta}\cdot\epsilon+\frac{3}{2}(a^{-1}\not{\nabla}a)\cdot\delta-\widehat{\kappa}\cdot(a^{-1}\not{\nabla}a),\\
{\not{\sdiv}\,}\widehat{\kappa}&=\frac{1}{2}(-\underline{\beta}+\beta)-\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}\delta+\widehat{\theta}\cdot\epsilon-\frac{1}{2}\tr\theta\,\epsilon,\\
\not{\nabla}_{N}\widehat{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\tr\theta\,\widehat{\kappa}&=\frac{1}{4}(-\underline{\alpha}+\alpha)+\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\varepsilon+\frac{3}{2}\delta\,\widehat{\theta}+(a^{-1}\not{\nabla}a)\widehat{\otimes}\epsilon,\end{split}

(6.1)

where we recall

\displaystyle\kappa_{AB}=k_{AB},\qquad\epsilon_{A}=k_{AN},\qquad\delta=k_{NN},

and
\widehat{\kappa}
denotes the traceless part of
\kappa
.

Proof.

See (11.1.2) in
[
5
]
.
∎

Proposition 6.3
.

We have the following identities:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\delta

\displaystyle=-\phi^{-1}\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\phi+\rho+\delta^{2}-\eta\cdot\underline{\eta}+\eta\cdot\epsilon-\underline{\eta}\cdot\epsilon,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\epsilon

\displaystyle=-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\nabla_{N}\phi+\frac{1}{2}(\beta+\underline{\beta})+(a^{-1}\not{\nabla}a)\phi^{-1}\nabla_{N}\phi

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}(\eta-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\phi)\delta+(\eta-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\phi+\epsilon)\cdot\widehat{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\delta\,\epsilon,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\widehat{\kappa}

\displaystyle=-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\not{\nabla}\phi+\frac{1}{4}(\alpha+\underline{\alpha})-\delta\,\widehat{\kappa}+\epsilon\widehat{\otimes}\epsilon-(\eta-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\phi)\widehat{\otimes}\epsilon.

Proof.

See (11.4.2) in
[
5
]
.
∎

Proposition 6.4
.

We define the position vectorfield:

Z:=rN.

(6.2)

We denote
\pi
the deformation tensor of
Z
:

\pi_{ij}:=\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{D}_{e_{i}}Z,e_{j})+\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{D}_{e_{j}}Z,e_{i})\right),

(6.3)

and
{\widehat{\pi}}
its traceless part. Then, we have

\displaystyle{\widehat{\pi}}_{ij}

\displaystyle=2r\widehat{\theta}_{ij}+\frac{1}{3}ra^{-1}\widecheck{a\tr\theta}(g_{ij}-N_{i}N_{j})-ra^{-1}(N_{i}\not{\nabla}_{j}a+N_{j}\not{\nabla}_{i}a),

\displaystyle\tr\pi

\displaystyle=ra^{-1}(2a\tr\theta+\overline{a\tr\theta}),

\displaystyle\pi_{ij}

\displaystyle={\widehat{\pi}}_{ij}+\frac{1}{3}\tr\pi g_{ij}.

Proof.

See (4.1.3) in
[
5
]
.
∎

Proposition 6.5
.

We define the following
1
–form:

(i_{Z}k)_{i}:=k_{ij}Z^{j}.

(6.4)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\sdiv(i_{Z}k)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}{\widehat{\pi}}^{ij}k_{ij},

\displaystyle\curl(i_{Z}k)_{i}

\displaystyle=H_{ij}Z^{j}+\frac{1}{2}{\in_{i}}^{mn}\left({{\widehat{\pi}}_{m}}^{j}-ra^{-1}(N_{m}\not{\nabla}^{j}a-N^{j}\not{\nabla}_{m}a)\right)k_{nj}.

Proof.

See (11.2.1) in
[
5
]
.
∎

Proposition 6.6
.

Let
\xi
be a
1
–form on
\Sigma
satisfying

\displaystyle\sdiv\xi

\displaystyle=D(\xi),

\displaystyle\curl\xi

\displaystyle=A(\xi).

Then, the following estimate holds for all
|p|<1
:

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}|\nabla\xi|^{2}+r^{p-2}|\xi|^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}\left(|A(\xi)|^{2}+|D(\xi)|^{2}+|\sRic||\xi|^{2}\right).

Proof.

Note that we have

\displaystyle\sdiv(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)=\nabla^{i}(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi_{i})=r^{\frac{p}{2}}D(\xi)+\frac{p}{2}r^{\frac{p-2}{2}}N(r)\xi_{N}.

We also have

\displaystyle\curl(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)_{i}

\displaystyle=r^{\frac{p}{2}}A(\xi)_{i}+{\in_{i}}^{lm}\nabla_{l}(r^{\frac{p}{2}})\xi_{m}

\displaystyle=r^{\frac{p}{2}}A(\xi)_{i}+\frac{p}{2}{\in_{i}}^{Nm}r^{\frac{p-2}{2}}N(r)\xi_{m},

which implies

\displaystyle\curl(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)=r^{\frac{p}{2}}A(\xi)+\frac{p}{2}r^{\frac{p-2}{2}}N(r)(-\xi_{2},\xi_{1},0).

Applying Proposition
4.18
, we deduce that for any constant
\delta_{0}>0

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{p^{2}(1+\delta_{0})}{4}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p-2}N(r)^{2}|\xi|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\delta_{0}}\right)\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}\left(|A(\xi)|^{2}+|D(\xi)|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}\sRic^{ij}\xi_{i}\xi_{j}.

Recalling that
N(r)=1+O(\varepsilon)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)|^{2}-\frac{p^{2}(1+\delta_{0})(1+O(\varepsilon))}{4}\frac{|r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi|^{2}}{r^{2}}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}\left(|A(\xi)|^{2}+|D(\xi)|^{2}+|\sRic||\xi|^{2}\right).

Note that we have for
\delta_{0}
and
\varepsilon
small enough

\displaystyle\frac{p^{2}(1+\delta_{0})(1+O(\varepsilon))}{4}<c_{H}^{-2}-\delta_{0},

where
c_{H}=2+O(\varepsilon)
is defined in Proposition
4.15
. Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)|^{2}-(c_{H}^{-2}-\delta_{0})\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p-2}|\xi|^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}\left(|A(\xi)|^{2}+|D(\xi)|^{2}+|\sRic||\xi|^{2}\right).

Applying Proposition
4.15
, we deduce

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla(r^{\frac{p}{2}}\xi)|^{2}+r^{p-2}|\xi|^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{p}\left(|A(\xi)|^{2}+|D(\xi)|^{2}+|\sRic||\xi|^{2}\right).

Recalling that
|\nabla r|=1+O(\varepsilon)
, this concludes the proof of Proposition
6.6
.
∎

6.2
Estimates for
\epsilon
and
\delta

Proposition 6.7
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s}[\nabla\epsilon,\nabla\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{F}_{s-2}[\epsilon,\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{D}_{s-2}[\epsilon,\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}_{0}.

Proof.

We have from Propositions
6.4
and
6.5

\displaystyle\sdiv(i_{Z}k)

\displaystyle=r\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

\displaystyle\curl(i_{Z}k)

\displaystyle=i_{Z}H+r\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

Applying Proposition
6.6
with
p=s-2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\;\;\;\,\,\,\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-4}|i_{Z}k|^{2}+r^{s-2}|\nabla i_{Z}k|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-2}|i_{Z}H|^{2}+r^{s}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+r^{s-2}|\sRic||i_{Z}k|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s}|(\beta,\sigma,\underline{\beta})|^{2}+r^{s}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+r^{s}|E||\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(r^{s-2}|r^{1-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{1-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}+|E|_{2,S}|r^{\frac{s}{2}}\Gamma_{g}|_{4,S}|r^{\frac{s}{2}}\Gamma_{g}|_{4,S}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{1}\left(r^{s-2}\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{2(s-1)}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\right)du+\int_{1}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{u^{s}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining with (
6.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-2}[\epsilon,\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{F}_{s}[\nabla\epsilon,\nabla\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Applying Proposition
4.16
, we deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}[\epsilon,\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.7
.
∎

Proposition 6.8
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s}^{1}[(\not{\nabla},\not{\nabla}_{N})\epsilon,(\not{\nabla},\not{\nabla}_{N})\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\epsilon,\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{D}_{s-1,1}[\not{\nabla}_{N}\epsilon,\not{\nabla}_{N}\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Proposition
6.5

\displaystyle\sdiv(i_{Z}k)

\displaystyle=r\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

\displaystyle\curl(i_{Z}k)

\displaystyle=i_{Z}H+r\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

Commuting with
r\not{\nabla}_{A}
, we obtain
21
21

21

See (6.119) in
[
1
]
for the precise equations and its derivation.

\displaystyle\sdiv(r\not{\nabla}_{A}(i_{Z}k))

\displaystyle=r\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}^{(1)},

\displaystyle\curl(r\not{\nabla}_{A}(i_{Z}k))

\displaystyle=r\not{\nabla}_{A}(i_{Z}H)+r\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}.

Applying Proposition
6.6
with
p=s-2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\;\;\;\,\,\,\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s}|\nabla\not{\nabla}_{A}i_{Z}k|^{2}+r^{s-2}|\not{\nabla}_{A}i_{Z}k|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s}|\not{\nabla}i_{Z}H|^{2}+r^{s}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}|^{2}+r^{s}|\sRic||\not{\nabla}i_{Z}k|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s}|(\beta,\sigma,\underline{\beta})^{(1)}|^{2}+r^{s}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+r^{s}|E||\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|,

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(|r^{1-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{s}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}+|E|_{2,S}|r^{\frac{s}{2}}\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{4,S}|r^{\frac{s}{2}}\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{4,S}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\right)du+\int_{1}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{u^{s}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining with Proposition
6.7
, we infer

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s}^{1}[(\not{\nabla},\not{\nabla}_{N})\epsilon,(\not{\nabla},\not{\nabla}_{N})\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Applying Proposition
4.16
, we deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\epsilon,\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Finally, we have from Proposition
6.2

\displaystyle{\not{\sdiv}\,}\epsilon

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{N}\delta-\frac{3}{2}\tr\theta\,\delta+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{N}\epsilon+\tr\theta\,\epsilon

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\beta-\underline{\beta})+\not{\nabla}\delta+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{b},

which implies

\displaystyle|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{N}(\epsilon,\delta)|_{p,S}\lesssim|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\beta,\underline{\beta})|+|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\not{\nabla}\epsilon,\not{\nabla}\delta)|_{p,S}+|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|_{p,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+2}{2}}u^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Hence, we obtain

\mathcal{D}_{s-1,1}[\not{\nabla}_{N}\epsilon,\not{\nabla}_{N}\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.8
.
∎

6.3
Estimate for
\widehat{\kappa}

Proposition 6.9
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-2}^{2}[\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{F}_{s}^{1}[\not{\nabla}_{N}\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{D}_{s-3,1}^{1}[\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\mathcal{D}_{-1,s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{N}\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

(6.5)

Moreover, we have

\mathcal{D}_{-1,s-1}[\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

(6.6)

Remark 6.10
.

Note that (
6.6
) implies that
\widehat{\kappa}
decays better than
\not{{\mathfrak{d}}}\widehat{\kappa}
.

Proof.

We have from Proposition
6.2

\displaystyle{\not{\sdiv}\,}\widehat{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(-\underline{\beta}+\beta)-\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}\delta-\frac{1}{2}\tr\theta\,\epsilon+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{N}\widehat{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\tr\theta\,\widehat{\kappa}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}(-\underline{\alpha}+\alpha)+\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\varepsilon+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

Applying Propositions
4.12
and
6.8
, we immediately obtain (
6.5
). Next, applying Lemma
4.24
, we have for
p\in[2,4]
and
u\leq 1

\displaystyle|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\widehat{\kappa}|_{p,S(t,u)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lim_{u\to\infty}|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\widehat{\kappa}|_{p,S(t,u)}+\int_{-\infty}^{u}\left(|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}(\alpha,\underline{\alpha},\not{\nabla}\epsilon)|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|_{p,S}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s}}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}},

and similarly for
1\leq u\leq u_{c}(t)

\displaystyle|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\widehat{\kappa}|_{p,S(t,u)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lim_{u\to u_{c}(t)}|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\widehat{\kappa}|_{p,S(t,u)}+\int_{u}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}(\alpha,\underline{\alpha},\not{\nabla}\epsilon)|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|_{p,S}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u^{s}}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}.

Hence, we obtain (
6.6
). This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.9
.
∎

6.4
Estimate for the lapse function
\phi

We have from (
2.5
) and (
4.1
) that

\Delta\phi=\phi|k|^{2},\qquad\quad\lim_{u\to-\infty}\phi=1.

In view of the maximum principle and Harnarck inequality
22
22

22

See for example Theorem 3.1 in
[
8
]
for the maximum principle and Theorem 8.20 in
[
8
]
for Harnarck inequality.
, we infer

\displaystyle 0<\phi\leq 1.

Thus, we can define the following negative scalar function:

\varphi:=\log\phi.

(6.7)

Lemma 6.11
.

We have the following equation for
\varphi
:

\Delta\varphi=|k|^{2}-|\nabla\varphi|^{2},\qquad\quad\lim_{u\to-\infty}\varphi=0.

Proof.

We have from (
6.7
)

\displaystyle\Delta\varphi=\nabla^{i}\nabla_{i}(\log\phi)=\nabla^{i}\left(\phi^{-1}\nabla_{i}\phi\right)=\phi^{-1}\Delta\phi-\phi^{-2}\nabla^{i}\phi\nabla_{i}\phi=|k|^{2}-|\nabla\varphi|^{2},

as stated. We also have from (
6.7
)

\lim_{u\to-\infty}\varphi=\log\left(\lim_{u\to-\infty}\phi\right)=0.

This concludes the proof of Lemma
6.11
.
∎

Proposition 6.12
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-2}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\mathcal{D}_{s-4}[\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

Recalling that
N(r)=1+r\Gamma_{b}
, we have from Lemma
6.11

\displaystyle r^{s-2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle=\nabla^{i}(r^{s-2}\varphi\nabla_{i}\varphi)-\nabla^{i}(r^{s-2})\varphi\nabla_{i}\varphi-r^{s-2}\varphi\Delta\varphi

\displaystyle=\sdiv(r^{s-2}\varphi\nabla\varphi)-(s-2)r^{s-3}N(r)\varphi N(\varphi)-r^{s-2}\varphi\left(|k|^{2}-|\nabla\varphi|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sdiv(r^{s-2}\varphi\nabla\varphi)-(s-2)r^{s-3}\varphi N(\varphi)+r^{s-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

We also have from (
4.5
)

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}2r^{s-3}\varphi N(\varphi)

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sdiv(r^{s-3}N)|\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-3}\sdiv(N)|\varphi|^{2}-(s-3)\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-4}N(r)|\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle=-(s-1)\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-4}|\varphi|^{2}+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}.

Combining the above identities, we infer

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(s-2)r^{s-3}\varphi N(\varphi)+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}

\displaystyle=\frac{(s-2)(s-1)}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-4}|\varphi|^{2}+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}r^{s+1}|r^{-\frac{2}{2}}\Gamma_{g}|_{2,S}|r^{-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}|r^{-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{1}r^{s+1}\frac{\varepsilon}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{r{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{r{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}+\int_{1}^{u_{c}(t)}r^{s+1}\frac{\varepsilon}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{ru^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{r^{\frac{s-1}{2}}u}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{1}\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}du+\int_{1}^{u_{c}(t)}\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

where we used the fact that

(s-2)(s-1)\leq 0.

Hence, we deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-2}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Applying Proposition
4.16
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-4}[\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.12
.
∎

Proposition 6.13
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s}[\nabla^{2}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\mathcal{D}_{s-2}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Lemma
6.11

\displaystyle\sdiv(\nabla\varphi)=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},\qquad\curl(\nabla\varphi)=0.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\sdiv(r^{\frac{s}{2}}\nabla\varphi)=\nabla^{i}(r^{\frac{s}{2}}\nabla_{i}\varphi)=\frac{s}{2}r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}N(r)\nabla_{N}\varphi+r^{\frac{s}{2}}\sdiv(\nabla\varphi)=r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}O(\nabla\varphi)+r^{\frac{s}{2}}\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

and similarly

\displaystyle\curl(r^{\frac{s}{2}}\nabla\varphi)=r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}O(\nabla\varphi).

Applying Propositions
4.16
and
6.12
, we infer

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla(r^{\frac{s}{2}}\nabla\varphi)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}+r^{s}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+|\sRic||r^{\frac{s}{2}}\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{u_{c}(t)}|r^{1-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{s}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{2}}E|_{2,S}|r^{\frac{s+1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{g}|_{4,S}|r^{\frac{s+1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{g}|_{4,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\right)du+\int_{1}^{u_{c}(t)}\left(\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{u^{s}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\right)du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining with Proposition
6.12
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s}[\nabla^{2}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Finally, applying Proposition
4.16
, we deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.13
.
∎

Proposition 6.14
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{1}_{s}[\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{F}_{s-2}^{2}[\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{F}_{s-4}^{3}[\varphi]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-1,1}[\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{D}_{s-4}^{2}[\varphi]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Lemma
6.11

\displaystyle\Delta\varphi=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

which implies

\displaystyle\Delta(r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}\varphi)

\displaystyle=\Delta(r^{\frac{s-2}{2}})\varphi+2\nabla^{i}(r^{\frac{s-2}{2}})\nabla_{i}\varphi+r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}\Delta\varphi

\displaystyle=r^{\frac{s-6}{2}}O(\varphi)+r^{\frac{s-4}{2}}O(\nabla\varphi)+r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

Applying Proposition
4.20
, we deduce

\displaystyle\;\;\;\,\,\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{4}|\not{\nabla}^{3}(r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}\varphi)|^{2}+r^{4}|\not{\nabla}^{2}\nabla_{N}(r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}\varphi)|^{2}+r^{4}|\not{\nabla}\nabla_{N}^{2}(r^{\frac{s-2}{2}}\varphi)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{s-4}|\varphi|^{2}+r^{s-2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}+r^{s}|\not{\nabla}\nabla\varphi|^{2}+r^{s}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{u_{c}(t)}|r^{1-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{s}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\Gamma_{w}^{(1)}|^{2}_{4,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{1}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-1}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}}du+\int_{1}^{u_{c}(t)}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u^{s-1}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining with Proposition
6.13
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{1}_{s}[\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{F}_{s-2}^{2}[\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{F}_{s-4}^{3}[\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Applying Proposition
4.16
, we infer

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\nabla_{N}\varphi]+\mathcal{D}_{s-4}^{2}[\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Finally, we have from Lemma
6.11

\displaystyle\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\varphi=-g^{AB}\nabla_{A}\nabla_{B}\varphi+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

which implies

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-1,1}[\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.14
.
∎

6.5
u
–decay estimates

In this section, we always assume that
u\geq 1
.

Proposition 6.15
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}[k]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}[\nabla k]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\nabla^{2}k]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

{}^{i}\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-3,s+1}[k]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\;\,{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\nabla k]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We recall from Proposition
6.1

\displaystyle\sdiv k

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\curl k

\displaystyle=H,

\displaystyle\tr k

\displaystyle=0.

Commuting with the truncation function
f
defined in Definition
5.36
, we deduce

\displaystyle\sdiv\overset{\circ}{k}

\displaystyle=O(k)\nabla f,

\displaystyle\curl\overset{\circ}{k}

\displaystyle=\overset{\circ}{H}+O(k)\nabla f,

\displaystyle\tr\overset{\circ}{k}

\displaystyle=0.

Applying Proposition
4.19
, we infer

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla\overset{\circ}{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\overset{\circ}{H}|^{2}+|\nabla f|^{2}|k|^{2}+|\sRic||\overset{\circ}{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s}}+\int_{\frac{t}{2}\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+1}}+\int_{0\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}|r^{1-\frac{2}{2}}\sRic|_{2,S}|r^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\overset{\circ}{k}|_{4,S}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s}}+\int_{0\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}t^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s}},

and similarly

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla^{2}\overset{\circ}{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla\overset{\circ}{H}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}f|^{2}|k|^{2}+|\nabla f|^{2}|\nabla k|^{2}+|\sRic||\nabla k|^{2}+|\sRic|^{2}|k|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}+\int_{\frac{t}{2}\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+3}}+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+3}}+\int_{0\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{t^{\frac{3s+7}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{t^{2s+2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}},

which implies from Proposition
4.16
that

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\nabla\overset{\circ}{k}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Moreover, applying Proposition
4.15
, we infer

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}r^{-2}|\nabla\overset{\circ}{k}|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}.

Finally, applying Propositions
4.16
and
4.17
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\overset{\circ}{k}|_{\infty,S(t,u)}\lesssim\|\overset{\circ}{k}\|_{6,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\overset{\circ}{k}\|_{6,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla\overset{\circ}{k}\|_{2,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\overset{\circ}{k}\|_{2,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}.

Combining with Propositions
6.7
–
6.9
, this concludes the proof of Proposition
6.15
.
∎

Proposition 6.16
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-4,s}[\varphi]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\;{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\;\;{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}[\nabla^{2}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\nabla^{3}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

{}^{i}\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-3,s-1}[\varphi]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\;^{i}\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-3,s+1}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\nabla^{2}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We recall from Lemma
6.11

\displaystyle\sdiv(\nabla\varphi)=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w},\qquad\curl(\nabla\varphi)=0,

which implies

\displaystyle\sdiv(f\nabla\varphi)

\displaystyle=f\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}+\nabla f\cdot\nabla\varphi,

\displaystyle\curl(f\nabla\varphi)

\displaystyle=\nabla f\cdot\nabla\varphi.

Applying Proposition
4.19
and proceeding as in Proposition
6.15
, we deduce

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}f^{2}|\nabla^{2}\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}f^{2}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+|\nabla f|^{2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}+f^{2}|\sRic||\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}|\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}|\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}+|\sRic|_{2,S}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}_{4,S}+\int_{\frac{t}{2}\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s}}.

We also have from Proposition
4.19

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}f^{2}|\nabla^{3}\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\nabla(f\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w})|^{2}+|\nabla(\nabla f\cdot\nabla\varphi)|^{2}+|\sRic||\nabla(f\nabla\varphi)|^{2}+|\sRic|^{2}|f\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t}}f^{2}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\nabla\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+|\nabla f|^{2}|\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}f|^{2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}+|\nabla f|^{2}|\nabla^{2}\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\sRic||\nabla f|^{2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}+f^{2}|\sRic||\nabla^{2}\varphi|^{2}+f^{2}|\sRic|^{2}|\nabla\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}|\Gamma_{w}|^{2}_{4,S}|\nabla\Gamma_{w}|_{4,S}^{2}+|\sRic|_{2,S}|\nabla^{2}\varphi|_{4,S}^{2}+|\sRic|^{2}_{4,S}|\nabla\varphi|_{4,S}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{t}{2}\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}r^{-2}|\Gamma_{w}|^{2}_{4,S}|\Gamma_{w}|^{2}_{4,S}+r^{-4}|\nabla\varphi|_{2,S}^{2}+r^{-2}|\nabla^{2}\varphi|_{2,S}^{2}+r^{-2}|\sRic|_{2,S}|\nabla\varphi|_{4,S}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}+\int_{0\leq r\leq\frac{3t}{4}}\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{t^{2s+2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+2}}.

Applying Proposition
4.15
, we deduce

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-4,s}[\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}[\nabla\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}[\nabla^{2}\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s+2}[\nabla^{3}\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Thus, we have from Proposition
4.16

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-4,s}[\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\nabla^{2}\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Finally, applying Propositions
4.16
and
4.17
, we obtain

\displaystyle|\nabla\overset{\circ}{\varphi}|_{\infty,S(t,u)}\lesssim\|\nabla\overset{\circ}{\varphi}\|_{6,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\overset{\circ}{\varphi}\|_{6,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla^{2}\overset{\circ}{\varphi}\|_{2,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{3}\overset{\circ}{\varphi}\|_{2,\Sigma_{t}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},

which implies

{}^{i}\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-3,s}[\nabla\overset{\circ}{\varphi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining with Propositions
6.12
–
6.14
, this concludes the proof of Proposition
6.16
.
∎

6.6
Time derivative estimates in the interior region

Proposition 6.17
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\sup_{p\in[-2,s-1]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\delta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\epsilon]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\sup_{p\in[-2,-1]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-p}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Proposition
6.3

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\delta

\displaystyle=-\phi^{-1}\nabla_{N}\nabla_{N}\phi+\rho+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\epsilon

\displaystyle=-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\nabla_{N}\phi+\frac{1}{2}(\beta+\underline{\beta})+\Gamma_{b}\cdot\Gamma_{w},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{T}\widehat{\kappa}

\displaystyle=-\phi^{-1}\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\not{\nabla}\phi+\frac{1}{4}(\alpha+\underline{\alpha})+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

Combining with Proposition
6.14
, we infer

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{s-1,1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\delta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{s-1,1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\epsilon]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-1,s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Moreover, we have from Proposition
6.16

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\delta,\not{\nabla}_{T}\epsilon,\not{\nabla}_{T}\widehat{\kappa}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
6.17
.
∎

Proposition 6.18
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\sup_{p\in[-2,s-2]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-2-p}[T\phi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\sup_{p\in[-2,s-1]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-p}[\nabla_{T}\nabla\phi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We first introduce the spacetime scaling operator

S:=\underline{t}\,T+Z,\qquad\underline{t}:=r+u,

(6.8)

where
Z=rN
is defined in (
6.2
). Commuting
S
with (
2.5
), we obtain
23
23

23

See (12.0.5f) in
[
5
]
for the precise equation and its derivation.

\displaystyle\Delta(S\phi)=2\phi\widehat{\kappa}^{AB}(\phi\,\underline{t}\sRic_{AB}+r\nabla_{N}\widehat{\kappa}_{AB})+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{w}.

(6.9)

Moreover, we have from Proposition
6.2

\displaystyle\nabla_{N}\widehat{\kappa}=-\frac{1}{4}\underline{\alpha}+r^{-1}\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}.

Combining with Propositions
2.20
and
2.21
, we have

\displaystyle 2\phi\widehat{\kappa}^{AB}(\phi\,\underline{t}\sRic_{AB}+r\nabla_{N}\widehat{\kappa}_{AB})

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\left(\underline{t}E-\frac{1}{4}r\underline{\alpha}+\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}\right)

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{w}\cdot(u\,\underline{\alpha})+\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)},

where we used
u\,\underline{\alpha}\in\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}
. Injecting it into (
6.9
), we infer

\displaystyle\Delta(S\phi)=\Gamma_{w}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}.

Thus, we deduce that
S\phi
satisfies a similar equation as
\varphi
. Proceeding as in Propositions
6.12
,
6.13
and
6.16
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{p\in[-3,s-4]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-4-p}[S\phi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\sup_{p\in[-2,s-2]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-2-p}[\nabla(S\phi)]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Next, we recall from Propositions
6.14
and
6.16

\displaystyle\sup_{p\in[-2,s-2]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-2-p}[\nabla\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\sup_{p\in[-2,s-3]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-2-p}[\nabla\nabla_{Z}\varphi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining the above estimates, we infer

\displaystyle\sup_{p\in[-2,s-4]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-4-p}[\underline{t}\,T\phi]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\sup_{p\in[-2,s-3]}{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{p,s-2-p}[\nabla(\underline{t}\,T\phi)]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Recalling that
\underline{t}=r+u
, this concludes the proof of Proposition
6.18
.
∎

Combining Propositions
6.12
–
6.18
, this concludes the proof of Theorem
3.9
.

7
Null connection estimates (Theorem
3.10
)

In this section, we apply Lemma
4.24
to prove Theorem
3.10
. Throughout this section, we always assume that
p\in[2,4]
and we denote

\displaystyle S:=S(t,u),\qquad S_{2}:=S(2,u),\qquad S_{c}:=\lim_{t\to t_{c}(u)}S(t,u).

7.1
Null transport equations

The following equations will be used repeatedly in Section
7
.

Proposition 7.1
.

We have the following transport equations:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}{\not{U}}+\frac{3}{2}\tr\chi\,{\not{U}}

\displaystyle=-\tr\chi\,\beta-\widehat{\chi}\cdot{\not{U}}-\not{\nabla}|\widehat{\chi}|^{2}+\tr\chi\,\widehat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\eta}-2|\widehat{\chi}|^{2}\eta,

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mu+\tr\chi\,\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi\left(-{\not{\sdiv}\,}\underline{\eta}-\wideparen{\rho}-|\underline{\eta}|^{2}\right)+\widehat{\chi}\cdot\not{\nabla}\widehat{\otimes}\eta

\displaystyle+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot{\not{U}}-\frac{1}{4}\tr\underline{\chi}|\widehat{\chi}|^{2}+2\eta\cdot\widehat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\eta}-2\eta\cdot\beta.

Proof.

See (13.1.6c) and (13.1.11) in
[
5
]
.
∎

Proposition 7.2
.

We have the following equations:

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\eta+\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi\,\eta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi\,\underline{\eta}-\widehat{\chi}\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta})-\beta,

\displaystyle{\not{\sdiv}\,}\eta

\displaystyle=-\mu-\wideparen{\rho},

\displaystyle{\not{\curl}\,}\eta

\displaystyle=\wideparen{\sigma},

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\tr\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\tr\chi)^{2}

\displaystyle=-2\omega\tr\chi-|\widehat{\chi}|^{2},

\displaystyle{\not{\sdiv}\,}\widehat{\chi}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}{\not{U}}-\beta-\zeta\cdot\widehat{\chi}.

Proof.

It follows directly from Proposition
2.17
, (
3.2
) and (
3.3
).
∎

7.2
Estimate for
{\not{U}}

Proposition 7.3
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s}[{\not{U}}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Proposition
7.1

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}{\not{U}}+\frac{3}{2}\tr\chi\,{\not{U}}=-\tr\chi\,\beta+r^{-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we obtain for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S_{2}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\beta|_{p,S}+|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-3}{2}}}+\int_{|u|}^{r}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{r^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s-3}{2}}},

which implies

\displaystyle{{}^{e}\mathcal{D}}_{s}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}.

(7.1)

Note that we have from (
4.1
)

\displaystyle\lim_{t\to t_{c}(u)}|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}=\lim_{t\to t_{c}(u)}r^{\frac{5}{2}}|r^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}\lesssim\lim_{r\to 0}\frac{\varepsilon r^{\frac{5}{2}}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}=0.

Thus, for any
S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{e}V}
, we have from Lemma
4.24

\displaystyle|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\beta|_{p,S}+|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{t^{s-1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s-3}{2}}},

which implies

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{s+3}{2}-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{e}V}.

(7.2)

Moreover, for any
S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{i}V}
, we have from Lemma
4.24

\displaystyle|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{3-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\beta|_{p,S}+|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{\frac{3}{2}}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}r^{\frac{3}{2}}}{u^{\frac{2s+1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{\frac{5}{2}}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}},

where we used
|t_{c}(u)-t|\lesssim r
for any
S(t,u)\subseteq{{}^{i}_{i}V}
. Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{p}}{\not{U}}|_{p,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}},\qquad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{i}V}.

(7.3)

Combining (
7.2
) and (
7.3
), we infer
24
24

24

Recall that
u\lesssim r
in
{{}^{i}_{e}V}
and
r\lesssim u
in
{{}^{i}_{i}V}
.

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{2,s-2}[{\not{U}}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{s}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Combining with (
7.1
), this concludes the proof of Proposition
7.3
.
∎

Proposition 7.4
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},\quad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{i}V},\qquad\qquad|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},\quad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{e}V}.

We also have

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[{\not{U}}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{E}}_{0,s}^{1}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad\mathcal{F}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[{\not{U}}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}^{1}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

where
{\delta_{B}}
is defined in (
3.4
).

Proof.

We have from Propositions
4.5
and
7.1

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}})+\frac{3}{2}\tr\chi(r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}})=O(\not{\nabla}\beta)+\Gamma_{g}\cdot({\not{U}}^{(1)},\beta^{(1)})+r^{-1}\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we obtain for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle|r^{3}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{3}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S_{2}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|r\beta^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|\Gamma_{g}|_{\infty,S}|r^{2}{\not{U}}^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|r\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{2,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-3}{2}}}+\left(\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}r^{s}|\beta^{(1)}|_{2,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}r^{2-s}dr\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}r^{\frac{s-3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s-3}{2}}},

which implies

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{s+3}{2}}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{e}V}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{s+3}{2}}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{e}V}.

Next, we have from Lemma
4.24
that for any
S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{i}V}

\displaystyle|r^{3}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{3}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r\beta^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|\Gamma_{g}|_{\infty,S}|r^{2}{\not{U}}^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|r\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{2,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r^{-1}|\beta^{(1)}|_{2,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r^{3}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|\Gamma_{g}|_{\infty,S}|r^{2}{\not{U}}^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|r\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{4,S}|\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{4,S},

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{2}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon r}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}r^{2}}{u^{s+2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{2}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},

where we used Proposition
5.39
at the third step. Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},\qquad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{i}_{i}V}.

Combining the above estimates, we deduce

\displaystyle|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},\quad\,\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{i}V},\qquad\qquad|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},\quad\forall\;S\subseteq{{}^{e}V}.

(7.4)

Thus, we obtain for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle\int_{{C_{u}^{V}}}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|^{2}\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}^{2}dt\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+1}}dt\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u^{s}},

and for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle\int_{{C_{u}^{V}}}r^{s-{\delta_{B}}}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|^{2}\lesssim\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}r^{s-{\delta_{B}}}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}^{2}\lesssim\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{r^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\delta_{B}}}.

Hence, we deduce

\mathcal{E}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad\quad{{}^{i}\mathcal{E}}_{0,s}^{1}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

(7.5)

Next, we have from (
7.4
) that for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}(-\infty,u)}r^{s-{\delta_{B}}}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|^{2}\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{r^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}du\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\delta_{B}}},

and similarly for
1\leq u_{1}\leq u_{2}\leq u_{c}(t)

\int_{\Sigma_{t}(u_{1},u_{2})}r^{s-{\delta_{B}}}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|^{2}\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}du\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{\delta_{B}}}.

Finally, we have from Lemma
4.24
that for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r^{3}\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r|\beta^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|\Gamma_{g}|_{\infty,S}|r^{2}{\not{U}}^{(1)}|_{2,S}+|r\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}\cdot\Gamma_{g}^{(1)}|_{2,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r^{-1}|\beta^{(1)}|^{2}_{2,S}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r^{3}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}r^{2}}{t^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{2}E_{-1}^{1}[\beta](u)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{2}}{u^{s}},

which implies

|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|_{2,S}\lesssim E_{-1}^{1}[\beta](u)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{s}}.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}|r\not{\nabla}{\not{U}}|^{2}_{2,S}\lesssim B_{0}^{1}[\beta](u_{1},u_{2})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{2s-1}}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}.

Combining the above estimates, we deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[{\not{U}}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}^{1}[{\not{U}}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

(7.6)

This concludes the proof of Proposition
7.4
.
∎

7.3
Estimate for
\mu

Proposition 7.5
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1,s-1}[\mu]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\mu]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Proposition
7.1

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\mu+\tr\chi\,\mu=r^{-1}O(\rho,\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta})+r^{-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we deduce for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S_{2}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}(\rho,\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta})|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}.

Note that we have from (
4.1
)

\displaystyle|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S_{c}}=\lim_{t\to t_{c}(u)}|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S}\lesssim\lim_{r\to 0}\frac{\varepsilon r^{\frac{3}{2}}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}=0.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we have for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}(\rho,\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta})|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{t^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}.

We also have for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim|r^{2-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}(\rho,\not{\nabla}\underline{\eta})|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{\frac{1}{2}}}{t^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}r^{\frac{1}{2}}}{t^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{\frac{3}{2}}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}},

which implies

|r^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{p}}\mu|_{p,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}.

Combining the above estimates, we infer

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1,s-1}[\mu]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\mu]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
7.5
.
∎

Proposition 7.6
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[\mu]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}^{1}[\mu]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Propositions
4.5
and
7.1

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(r\not{\nabla}\mu)+\tr\chi(r\not{\nabla}\mu)=O(\not{\nabla}\rho)+O(\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\eta})+r^{-1}(\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b})^{(2)}.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we infer for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle|r^{2}\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}\lesssim|r^{2}\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S_{2}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|X|_{2,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|X|_{2,S},

where we denoted

\displaystyle X:=\left(r\not{\nabla}\rho,r\not{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\eta},(\Gamma_{b}\cdot\Gamma_{g})^{(2)}\right),

which satisfies

\mathcal{F}_{s}[X]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\qquad{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s+2}[X]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

(7.7)

Hence, we obtain for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle|r^{2}\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-1}}+\left(\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|X|_{2,S}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-1}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}r^{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}|X|_{2,S}^{2}\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}r^{1-s+{\delta_{B}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-1}}+r^{2-s+{\delta_{B}}}E_{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}[X](u),

which implies

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{u}|r^{\frac{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}{2}}\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{r^{s-2+{\delta_{B}}}{\langle u\rangle}^{s-1}}+B_{s-{\delta_{B}}}[X](u)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}+\int_{2}^{t}\frac{dt}{(t+|u|)^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{s}|X|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\delta_{B}}}.

Moreover, we have from Lemma
4.24
that for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r^{2}\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}^{2}\lesssim\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|X|_{2,S}\right)^{2}\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r^{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}|X|_{2,S}^{2}\int_{0}^{r}r^{1-s+{\delta_{B}}}\lesssim r^{2-s+{\delta_{B}}}E_{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}[X],

which implies for
1\leq u_{1}\leq u_{2}\leq u_{c}(t)
25
25

25

Recall that
t\lesssim r
in
{{}^{i}_{e}V}
while
t\lesssim u
in
{{}^{i}_{i}V}
.

\displaystyle\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}|r^{\frac{s+2-{\delta_{B}}}{2}}\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{{}^{i}_{e}V}r^{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}|X|^{2}+\int_{{}^{i}_{i}V}r^{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}|X|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}t^{-1-{\delta_{B}}}\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}\cap{{}^{i}_{e}V}}r^{s}|X|^{2}+\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}\cap{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{s-1-{\delta_{B}}}|X|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}+\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{\delta_{B}}}.

Thus, we obtain

\mathcal{F}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[\mu]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Similarly, we have for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}^{2}\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}r^{-1}|X|^{2}_{2,S}=E_{-1}[X](u),

which implies for
1\leq u_{1}\leq u_{2}\leq u_{c}(t)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}|r\not{\nabla}\mu|_{2,S}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{{{}^{i}_{e}V}}r^{-1}|X|^{2}+\int_{{}^{i}_{i}V}r^{-1}|X|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{1+{\delta_{B}}}}\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}\cap{{}^{i}_{e}V}}r^{\delta_{B}}|X|^{2}+\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}dt\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}\cap{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{-1}|X|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s-{\delta_{B}}}}\frac{1}{u_{1}^{{\delta_{B}}}}+\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{t^{s+1}}dt

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}.

Combining the above estimates, we deduce

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[\mu]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{0,s}^{1}[\mu]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
7.6
.
∎

7.4
Estimates for
\tr\chi
,
\widehat{\chi}
and
\eta

Proposition 7.7
.

We have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{-1,s-1}^{1}[\eta]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}^{1}_{-3,s+1}[\eta]+\mathcal{F}_{s-2-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{2}[\eta]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}^{2}[\eta]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\widehat{\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-3,s+1}^{1}[\widehat{\chi}]+\mathcal{F}_{s-2-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{2}[\widehat{\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}^{2}[\widehat{\chi}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-3,s+1}^{1}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]+\mathcal{F}_{s-2-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{2}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}^{2}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-1,s-1}\left[\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-3,s+1}}\left[\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Proposition
7.2

\displaystyle{\not{\sdiv}\,}\eta=-\mu-\wideparen{\rho},\qquad\quad{\not{\curl}\,}\eta=\wideparen{\sigma}.

Applying Proposition
4.12
, we have

\displaystyle|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(r\not{\nabla})^{\leq 1}\eta|_{p,S}\lesssim|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}(\mu,\wideparen{\rho},\wideparen{\sigma})|_{p,S}.

Combining with Proposition
7.5
, we infer

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{-1,s-1}^{1}[\eta]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}^{1}_{-3,s+1}[\eta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Moreover, combining with Proposition
7.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-2-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{1}[\eta]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}^{1}[\eta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Similarly, we have from Proposition
7.2

\displaystyle{\not{\sdiv}\,}\widehat{\chi}=\frac{1}{2}{\not{U}}-\beta+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}.

Applying Proposition
4.12
, we deduce

\displaystyle|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(r\not{\nabla})^{\leq 1}\widehat{\chi}|_{p,S}\lesssim|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}({\not{U}},\beta,\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g})|_{p,S}.

Combining with Propositions
7.3
and
7.4
, we infer

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\widehat{\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-3,s+1}^{1}[\widehat{\chi}]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s-2-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{2}[\widehat{\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}^{2}[\widehat{\chi}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Similarly, we have from (
2.14
), (
3.2
) and Proposition
7.4

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{D}_{s-2}^{1}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-3,s+1}^{1}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]&\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2},\\
