\mathcal{F}_{s-2-{\delta_{B}},{\delta_{B}}}^{2}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{F}}_{-2,s}^{2}[\widecheck{\tr\chi}]&\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.\end{split}

(7.8)

Next, we recall from Proposition
7.2

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\tr\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\tr\chi)^{2}=-2\omega\tr\chi+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi\left(\frac{2}{r}\right)=-\frac{2e_{4}(r)}{r^{2}}+\frac{\tr\chi}{r}=\frac{\widecheck{\phi\tr\chi}}{\phi r}.

Taking the difference, we obtain

\not{\nabla}_{4}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)=r^{-1}O(\omega,\widecheck{\phi\tr\chi})+\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we infer for
u\leq 1

\displaystyle\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S_{2}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\omega,\widecheck{\phi\tr\chi})|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}+\int_{{\langle u\rangle}}^{r}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{r^{s}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}.

Noticing that

\displaystyle\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S_{c}}=\lim_{t\to t_{c}(u)}\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S}\lesssim\lim_{r\to 0}\frac{\varepsilon r}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}=0,

we have for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\omega,\widecheck{\phi\tr\chi})|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}.

We also have for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\left(\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right)\right|_{p,S_{c}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}(\omega,\widecheck{\phi\tr\chi})|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{g}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}+\frac{r\varepsilon^{2}}{t^{s+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}.

Combining the above estimates and (
7.8
), we infer

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-1,s-1}\left[\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}^{(\infty)}_{-3,s+1}}\left[\tr\chi-\frac{2}{r}\right]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
7.7
.
∎

7.5
Estimate for
\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta

Proposition 7.8
.

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-1,s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta]+{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Proof.

We have from Propositions
7.2
and
4.6

\displaystyle\not{\nabla}_{4}(\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta)+\frac{1}{2}\tr\chi(\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta)=r^{-1}O(\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\eta})-\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta+r^{-1}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}.

We have from (
2.14
) that in
{{}^{i}V}

\displaystyle|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\eta}|_{p,S}\lesssim|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\epsilon|_{p,S}+|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\nabla\phi|_{p,S}\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}u^{\frac{1}{2}}t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}.

Applying Lemma
4.24
, we infer for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\eta}|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta|_{p,S}+|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}u^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{t^{\frac{s+3}{2}}u^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}}},

which implies

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-1,s+1}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

Similarly, applying Lemma
4.24
, we obtain for
u\geq 1

\displaystyle|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\underline{\eta}|_{p,S}+|r^{1-\frac{2}{p}}\not{\nabla}_{T}\beta|_{p,S}+|r^{-\frac{2}{p}}\Gamma_{g}\cdot\Gamma_{b}^{(1)}|_{p,S}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}t^{\frac{s+1}{2}}u^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{t^{\frac{s+3}{2}}u^{\frac{s-1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}}{r^{\frac{1}{2}}u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon_{0}r^{\frac{1}{2}}}{u^{\frac{s+2}{2}}},

which implies

\displaystyle{{}^{i}\mathcal{D}}_{-2,s+2}[\not{\nabla}_{T}\eta]\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}^{2}.

This concludes the proof of Proposition
7.8
.
∎

Combining Propositions
7.3
–
7.8
, this concludes the proof of Theorem
3.10
.

Appendix A A
Proof of Theorem
5.8

We prove the following lemmas, which directly imply Theorem
5.8
.

Lemma A.1
.

Let
p_{1},p_{2},p,\ell\leq s
and let
s>1
. Then, we have the following properties:

1.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{e}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

(A.1)

2.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell
and
2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+s
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{e}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}},

(A.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{e}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

(A.3)

3.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell-1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{e}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

(A.4)

Proof.

Throughout the proof, we always denote
V:={{}^{e}V}
and
0<\delta\ll s-1
a constant sufficiently small.

We first assume that
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{V}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}\frac{1}{r^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1}{2}-p}}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{\frac{p_{2}}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{V}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{1}-p}{2}}}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{2}-p}{2}}}r^{\frac{p_{2}}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{V}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{p_{1}-p}}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}+\int_{V}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{p_{2}-p}}r^{p_{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{p_{1}-p}}\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}+\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{p_{2}-p}}\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{p_{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{p_{1}-p}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p_{1}}}+\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{p_{2}-p}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p_{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

We also have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\left(\int_{{C_{u}^{V}}}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{2p-p_{1}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|_{4,S}^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-2}|r^{\frac{p_{2}+3}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{\ell+1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-2}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p_{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\ell}}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\frac{p_{2}+\ell}{2}}}\left({\langle u\rangle}^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1+\delta}\int_{2}^{t}r^{-1-\delta}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\frac{p_{2}+\ell}{2}}}{\langle u\rangle}^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1-\delta}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p-\delta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

Hence, we obtain (
A.1
).

Next, we assume that
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\left(\int_{{C_{u}^{V}}}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{2p-p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|_{4,S}^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1}|r^{\frac{p_{2}+2}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{\ell+1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p_{2}+1}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\ell}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\frac{p_{2}}{2}+1-\frac{\ell+1}{2}}}\left({\langle u\rangle}^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1+\delta}\int_{2}^{t}r^{-1-\delta}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p-\delta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

We also have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{V}\frac{\varepsilon}{r^{\frac{\ell+1}{2}-p}{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{V}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell-1-\delta}{2}}}\frac{1}{r^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell-\delta}{2}-p}}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{-\frac{1+\delta}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{\frac{p_{2}}{2}}r^{-\frac{1+\delta}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+s-1}{2}-p-\delta}}\int_{V}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{-\frac{1+\delta}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{\frac{p_{2}}{2}}r^{-\frac{1+\delta}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+s-1}{2}-p-\delta}}\left(\int_{V}r^{p_{1}}{\langle u\rangle}^{-1-\delta}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{V}r^{p_{2}}r^{-{1-\delta}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+s-1}{2}-p-\delta}}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{u}{\langle u\rangle}^{-1-\delta}\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}t^{-1-\delta}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{p_{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+s-1}{2}-p-\delta}}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}},

where we used
p_{1}+p_{2}+s-1>2p
. Hence, we obtain (
A.2
).

Proceeding as above, we deduce for
p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell>2p
and
p_{1}+p_{2}+s-1>2p

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}\cdot\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

We also have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\left(\int_{{C_{u}^{V}}}r^{p_{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{2p-p_{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}|^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}|_{4,S}^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1}|r^{\frac{p_{1}+2}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{\ell+1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}\left(\int_{2}^{t}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p_{1}+1}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\ell}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{-\infty}^{u}du\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}+1}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell}{2}-p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}\int_{-\infty}^{u}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s+1}{2}}}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

Hence, we obtain (
A.3
).

Finally, we assume that
2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{k}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{2}^{t}dt\left(\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{2p-p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{2}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{u}r^{2p-p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|_{4,S}^{2}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{2}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{u}r^{2p-p_{1}-p_{2}-\ell-1}|r^{\frac{p_{2}+2}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|_{4,S}^{2}|r^{\frac{\ell+1}{2}-\frac{2}{4}}\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|_{4,S}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{2}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}r^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1}{2}}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{u}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p_{2}+1}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\ell}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{2}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}r^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1-\delta}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{2s-p_{2}-\ell+\delta}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}{\langle u\rangle}^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}-\delta}{2}}\frac{1}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-\frac{p_{2}+1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

We also have

\displaystyle\int_{V}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{2}^{t}dt\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}}r^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}}{2}}\frac{\varepsilon}{r^{\frac{\ell+1}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{\frac{p_{2}}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}\int_{2}^{t}r^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1}{2}}dt\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{\frac{p_{2}}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}\int_{2}^{t}r^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1}{2}}dt\left(\int_{{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{p_{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}\int_{2}^{t}r^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1}{2}}dt\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-\ell}{2}}}{\langle u\rangle}^{p-\frac{p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell-1-\delta}{2}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}{\langle u\rangle}^{\frac{s-p_{2}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{{\langle u\rangle}^{s-p}}.

Hence, we obtain (
A.4
). This concludes the proof of Lemma
A.1
.
∎

Lemma A.2
.

Let
p_{1},p_{2},p,\ell\leq s
and
s>1
. Then, we have the following properties.

1.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell+1
, we have

\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{e}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(A.5)

2.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell
and
2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+s
, we have

\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{e}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(A.6)

3.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell
and
2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+s
, we have

\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{e}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(A.7)

4.

In the case
2p<p_{1}+p_{2}+\ell-1
, we have

\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{e}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(A.8)

Proof.

The proof follows directly by replacing
\int_{-\infty}^{u}du
with
\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du
and
\int_{2}^{t}dt
with
\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dt
in the proof of Lemma
A.1
.
∎

Lemma A.3
.

Let
0\leq p_{1},p_{2},p\leq s
,
\ell\leq s
and let
s>1
. Then, we have the following properties.

1.

We have

\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{i}V})\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(A.9)

2.

In the case
2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+s
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{i}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

(A.10)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{i}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

(A.11)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{1}_{p}\left[\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}},\mathbb{O}_{\ell}\cdot\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\right]({{}^{i}_{i}V})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

(A.12)

Proof.

Throughout the proof, we always denote
V:={{}^{i}_{i}V}
and
0<\delta\ll s-1
a constant small enough. Note that we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}\subseteq\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}},\qquad\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}\subseteq\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}\qquad\forall\;p_{1}\geq p_{2}\geq 0.

We also recall that

\displaystyle r\lesssim u,\,t\quad\mbox{ in }\;{{}^{i}_{i}V}.

We first have

\displaystyle\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{\varepsilon}{u^{\frac{s-2p+1}{2}}}du\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u^{\frac{3s-2p+1}{2}}}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

We also have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|&\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}u^{p}du\int_{C_{u}^{V}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|\\
&\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}u^{p}du\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{C_{u}^{V}}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}u^{p}du\frac{\varepsilon}{u^{\frac{s}{2}}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dt|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}_{4,S}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}_{4,S}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du\frac{\varepsilon}{u^{\frac{s-2p}{2}}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u^{s}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u^{s+2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du\frac{\varepsilon}{u^{\frac{s-2p}{2}}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u^{\frac{{2s+1}}{2}}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.\end{split}

(A.13)

Thus, we obtain (
A.9
).

In the rest of the proof, we always assume that

\displaystyle 2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+s.

(A.14)

We have for
p<\frac{s-1}{2}

\displaystyle\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}\frac{1}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{du}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\int_{C_{u}^{V}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{du}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u^{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s}}\frac{1}{u_{1}^{\frac{s-1}{2}-p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

and for
p\geq\frac{s-1}{2}
and
0<\delta\ll s-1
sufficiently small

\displaystyle\;\;\;\,\,\,\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}\frac{1}{u^{1+\delta}}r^{p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}\frac{1}{u^{1+\delta}}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{p_{1}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{p_{2}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{du}{u^{1+\delta}}\int_{C_{u}^{V}}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{1}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{1+\delta}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{2}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}\frac{du}{u^{1+\delta}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{1+\delta}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

where we used

\displaystyle\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{1}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}<p_{1},\qquad\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{2}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}<p_{2},

which follows from (
A.14
) and the smallness of
\delta
. Proceeding as in (
A.13
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

Thus, we obtain (
A.10
).

Next, we have for
p\leq\frac{p_{1}}{2}
and
0<\delta\ll s-1
sufficiently small

\displaystyle\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}\frac{r^{2p}}{u^{1+\delta}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}u^{1+\delta}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{1+\delta}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{2p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dtu^{1+\delta}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}_{4,S}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}_{4,S}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{u_{1}^{\frac{s-2p}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{u^{2s+1-\delta}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

and also for
p\geq\frac{p_{1}}{2}

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|&\lesssim\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{\frac{p_{1}}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|r^{p-\frac{p_{1}}{2}}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|\\
&\lesssim\left(\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}u^{-1-\delta}r^{p_{1}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}u^{2p-p_{1}+1+\delta}|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{u_{1}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}duu^{2p-p_{1}+1+\delta}\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dt|\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}|^{2}_{4,S}|\mathbb{E}_{p_{2}}|^{2}_{4,S}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{u_{1}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}duu^{2p-p_{1}+1+\delta}\int_{t_{c}(u)}^{t}dt\frac{\varepsilon^{4}}{t^{2s+2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{\frac{s-p_{1}}{2}}}\left(\int_{u_{1}}^{u_{2}}du\frac{1}{u^{2s-2p+p_{1}-\delta}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},\end{split}

(A.15)

where we used
2p+1<p_{1}+2s
.
26
26

26

Recalling that
p_{2}\leq s
and
p_{1}+p_{2}+s>2p+1
, we deduce that
2p+1<p_{1}+2s
.
Thus, we obtain (
A.11
).

Finally, we have for
p<\frac{s-1}{2}

\displaystyle\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}\frac{1}{u^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{\frac{s+1}{2}-p}}\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{u_{1}^{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

and also for
p\geq\frac{s-1}{2}
and
0<\delta\ll s-1
sufficiently small

\displaystyle\;\;\;\,\,\,\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}\frac{\varepsilon}{u^{1+\delta}}r^{p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}u^{-1-\delta}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{1}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{{{}^{i}_{i}V}}u^{-1-\delta}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{2}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{1+\delta}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{1}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{t_{c}(u_{1})}^{t}\frac{dt}{t^{1+\delta}}\int_{\Sigma_{t}^{V}}r^{\left(p+\delta-\frac{s-1}{2}\right)\frac{2p_{2}}{p_{1}+p_{2}}}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}^{(1)}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}},

where we used
2p+1<p_{1}+p_{2}+s
. Proceeding as in (
A.15
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{V_{i}}r^{p}|\mathbb{F}_{p_{1}}^{(1)}||\mathbb{O}_{\ell}^{(1)}||\mathbb{F}_{p_{2}}|\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{u_{1}^{s-p}}.

Thus, we obtain (
A.12
). This concludes the proof of Lemma
A.3
.
∎

Combining Lemmas
A.1
–
A.3
, this concludes the proof of Theorem
5.8
.

27

27

Note that the assumptions in Lemma
A.3
are weaker than those of Lemma
A.2
.
